# Low-rank spells — reviewed authored playtest manuscript

**Status: 136 complete authored procedures reviewed; LRX-01–05 fulfilled. Blink remains a nonoperative receipt.**

These complete authored procedures are approved for tabletop playtest under LRX-EXACT-2026-10-11. Their full original source records, class access and all 141 book bindings are in the companion JSON. The authored review index connects these 136 procedures with the 276 earlier reviewed entries. The older catalog and runtime selection remain unchanged; a complete adapter is not implemented. The 20 shared rules below are part of every applicable card. NEW labels identify source-authoring decisions; they do not claim those details were printed in the old sources.

The full-game flow review, human playtesting, GitHub push and later careful cosmology review remain separate. All original source bytes are preserved. Only the 136 authorized authored-review labels and provenance have been promoted after the finite corrections.

## Shared operating rules

### LR-G01 — Authority, access and cost

The selected ordinary identity and its actual classAccess row control school, rank, minimum class level and base RP. Preserve the entire classAccess object, every catalog/Core original, book binding, placeholder, absent/null distinction and additionalRpCost. Enough RP does not grant access or repertoire. An already completed explicit ordinary-spell access exception, such as the actual Druid Circle always-prepared table, retains its real acquisition/rank/preparation gates once its matching payload passes exact review. Record that particular grant and use its actual class progression/cost; do not rewrite ordinary classAccess, infer a new variant from a same-named class power, or approve a pending class feature here. Rank costs remain 0/2/3/5/7/10/13/17/21/26 for ranks 0–9, with the actual class engine, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and additional/maintenance costs separately applied. Oracle Revelations remain RC techniques; a same-named class, ancestry or recipe permission is not this ordinary spell. No default upcast or cantrip level scaling is added. Every direction below is authoring authority only; every existing source operational:null and pending status remains untouched until separate exact review.

### LR-G02 — Actual casting transaction

Use the selected source Action, Bonus Action, Reaction or longer casting time and actual V/S/F or M/F requirements. V requires the caster's own presently usable voice; recordings are not V. S and a real permitted focus obey the existing hand/access rules. Unpriced M/F permits the actual focus alternative and creates no invented costly/consumed material fee. Explicit physical substrates below are real target/work prerequisites, not inferred M costs. Observable legality is checked before commitment; hidden immunity/type/meaning does not become free diagnosis. At commitment spend the actual action/resources and update actual cast/cadence history before the existing counterspell chain; a countered, missed or resisted committed cast refunds nothing. Concentration begins under the existing rule and can end the old concentration even when the new cast is countered. Long casting uses actual uninterrupted work and governing interruption/Ready rules. No successful spell roll authenticates an asserted object, voice, teacher, corpse or world permission.

### LR-G03 — Native rolls and damage

Spell attacks use the actual owner's spell attack modifier versus AC: natural 1 misses, natural 20 hits and doubles damage dice of that attack, not flat modifiers or later ticks. Saves are binary total-versus-spell-DC, with no natural-die degree shift. A specified examination is an INT (Investigation) task against the creating caster's snapshotted spell DC: use Core degrees/natural shifts, then collapse Success/Critical Success versus Failure/Critical Failure unless an entry supplies distinct outcomes. For one simultaneous area effect, snapshot geometry and eligible targets before damage, roll each stated shared damage expression once, then apply each recipient's save and actual type defenses independently. Half damage floors each typed component once at the stated save stage. Follow the existing reduction, immunity/resistance/vulnerability, temporary/form buffer, actual HP loss, Wound, death and concentration pipeline. No descriptive Damage/Healing/Control tag supplies an additional attack, damage, healing or condition.

### LR-G04 — Clocks

A bare hostile one-round rider ends at the end of the target's next future regular turn, excluding an in-progress turn at application; a bare self/ally one-round benefit ends at the start of the caster's next future regular turn. N hostile rounds use the Nth such target end; N buff rounds use the Nth caster start. Outside initiative, those are 6N elapsed seconds. Keep the registered clock through initiative changes using the existing virtual-clock adapter; extra turns do not create expiry/tick opportunities. A NEW duration expressed in minutes/hours below uses an absolute elapsed deadline from completed resolution, including an absolute 60-second deadline for “1 minute”; the one-minute means neither an extra initial tick nor reset when initiative starts. Repeated round damage expressly uses future target regular-end checkpoints and its printed finite count, not one immediate tick plus that count. End-turn ticks/repeat saves precede their scheduled end-turn expiry; start expiry precedes voluntary actions. Suppression pauses eligibility/effect, not deadlines; scheduled suppressed ticks are spent, never queued. Entries that expressly use a named trigger instead of a generic clock keep that trigger.

### LR-G05 — Multiplicity, effect identity and present state

Keep the existing strongest same-stat flat bonus, same-family cover arbitration, advantage/disadvantage cancellation and one eligible bonus-die spell per roll. Independent class exceptions retain their exact scopes; this is not a universal strongest-effect rule. NEW default for these previously uncompleted spells: one active instance per caster per ordinary spell ID, unless a row expressly permits more; a successfully resolved replacement ends that caster's prior instance, but an illegal/countered attempt does not replace a nonconcentration instance. Different casters retain separate source records, clocks and restrictions; identical numeric bonuses never add. A current complete record is required for every competing effect. Instantaneous completed consequences are not active-instance stock. An entry explicitly limiting waiting triggers counts only its stated waiting state; its spent playback phase never refills or occupies a waiting slot. Continual Flame explicitly permits separate paid anchors, with only its per-anchor limit; do not impose the default one-caster limit on it. Loss, repair, transfer, suppression or a new database ID cannot renew an already consumed mark, scheduled tick, trigger, command budget or source resource. Removal of one source does not erase another's independent condition. Any change in actual target/custody state is logged; it is not a new casting.

### LR-G06 — Attack/check marks

A row expressly saying attack commitment reserves and spends its mark when the beneficiary commits a legal declared attack, before a roll; cancellation after that commitment and a miss do not refund it, while an illegal declaration that never commits does not spend it. Otherwise “next attack/check” marks spend on the next actual qualifying roll, including a miss/failure; unrelated rolls and passive procedures do not consume them. Mark recipients cannot choose to save a mark for a later preferred roll. A next-hit rider spends on the first qualifying hit, even if defenses reduce its damage to zero. Multiple competing next-roll marks eligible for the same event all spend, though only permitted strongest/advantage effects operate. Suppressed marks do not benefit or consume on an event while suppressed, but their deadline continues. Delivery attacks and later attacks consuming a delivered mark are different events.

### LR-G07 — Sight, illusions and meaning

An image is not an opaque physical barrier, creature, material, light source, safe floor, sound receiver or extra targetable body unless its row expressly creates that thing. Ordinary hearing needs a real acoustic path; sight needs the relevant illumination, range and unobstructed view. Knowing an illusion does not grant sight through a real wall. For visual/audio deceptions below that permit examination, one Action using a currently accessible relevant sense can make the LR-G03 examination; success identifies that cast as illusory for that observer, while failure permits another examination only after genuinely new evidence or method. Conclusive witnessed physical contradiction establishes the applicable false part without a roll. An ally's warning allows examination but does not globally switch every observer to knowing. These procedures do not compel belief, pursuit, an attack, trust or a sale. Current Invisible rules remain: invisibility is not automatic Hidden; observers who know location lose only the special invisibility-derived attack advantage/disadvantage, not unrelated concealment. Language samples reproduce what was heard, not meaning or credentials. Preserve LA's six-second aggregate voluntary-speech budget per numbered round and actual component/oath windows. Spell-created sounds are not the caster's later speech, but original recording speech and the caster's V are real speech.

### LR-G08 — Range, targets and geometry

Unless a specific row explicitly uses detection without sight, select an actually perceived eligible target or point within the literal Core range and with the existing line of effect. A target can be self within a creature-target range unless the source/row restricts it. “Willing” means current actual consent; an unwilling holder prevents a willing-only object spell without supplying an opposed-roll workaround. Detecting some magic is not necessarily perceiving a creature for targeting it. One target means one physical identity, not every same-named database row. NEW missing cone width is its length at its far end; missing line cross-section is five feet wide and five feet high. All source radii remain radii, not distances added to cast range. Area membership/cover are evaluated from the actual origin at that event; walls are not retroactively removed for a second pass. “Enemies” uses the caster's declared ordinary opposition set, not a supernatural alignment/intent detector. Caster/allies are included in indiscriminate areas. No range is silently replaced by an outside game's value.

### LR-G09 — Conditions and hard-control guard

Use actual Blinded, Deafened, Frightened, Prone, Restrained, Incapacitated, Paralyzed and Stunned definitions without importing another game. Paralyzed/Incapacitated and other hard Action denial obey the current source-character/target/encounter allowance shared across that owner's effects; only successfully established hard control spends it, once for the effect's full allowed window. A blocked hard portion supplies no invented substitute penalty; independently printed damage, Prone or another separately allowed portion can remain. A spell's repeat save ends only the stated source. Ending laughter, restraint or paralysis does not automatically stand a Prone target. NEW “flee” directions below require actual safe available movement away at the next regular-turn movement opportunity, using existing Speed/cost and leaving Actions to the target; they do not force Dash, expend a Reaction or require deliberate self-harm. If no safe farther legal location exists, no movement is forced and no action is lost. Panicked is explicitly bound only in IL-23, not added as a global condition. Haste's voluntary ending cost is separately addressed in R3-12.

### LR-G10 — Movement, falls and body continuity

Ordinary movement spends real movement cost and needs nonzero permitted Speed, legal support, actual space and the appropriate locomotion mode; changing a speed or mode does not reset spent cost. A spell-directed extra movement allowance expressly stated below supplies only that finite allowance. Teleportation relocates rather than traverses, does not itself provoke opportunity attacks and does not generically defeat a source that blocks relocation; no route is inferred through planes. Check destination occupancy, whole body/gear geometry and lawful carry/load. Supplying a point is not permission to materialize inside another body. Unsupported endings, launches and lost flight use the existing FallMarker and finite falling resolution, not a new immediate damage tick. Actual transformations retain original identity, own HP/resources, spent state, origin restrictions and body/equipment priority; no new monster sheet, harvest pool, HP refill, gear duplication or size-based ability increase. Actual equipment can be melded only by the stated form procedure, stays real weight/history and is unavailable while melded.

### LR-G11 — Summons, corpse assets and commands

Each row supplies its actual finite body, attack and lifetime rather than importing a monster. Use printed HP/AC before any new fallback. For the NEW profiles below, creating caster's class level/PB/ability at creation fixes any printed-derived maximum; attacks and saves use that same source owner's currently lawful spell attack/PB, not a transferee's or a monster's sheet. Unless stated, saves add owner's PB, ordinary ability/skill checks add 0, normal vision only, no special damage or condition immunity, no independent equipment proficiency or spellcasting. One such spell-created asset per caster per spell ID; the existing total companion/control budget still binds. Creation grants no free attack or movement. A command uses one Bonus Action on the owner's regular turn through actual visible/audible communication within 60 ft; no telepathy is invented. It acts in the attached phase after that whole turn, shares the owner's one command and one offensive companion/device allowance per numbered round, and has no independent refresh on extra turns. Uncommanded it can only ordinary Dodge and make defensive movement within speed; no automatic pursuit, work or offense. Loss of the channel means no new command, not free autonomy. A complete task needs its real appendages, capacity, instructions and paid time. At 0 HP, expiry, source dismissal (Action) or concentration end where required, the asset ends; summoned apparatus disappears without loot, corpses, ammunition, harvest or salvage. An actual corpse used as fuel retains its original finite matter/history and the stated terminal consumption; no source can reclassify it as newly obtained material. Keep Ranger's genuinely ordinary animal remains distinct from no-resource summons.

### LR-G12 — Suppression and trigger ordering

An ongoing magical object's target stays the same physical anchor; transfer or repair cannot duplicate/rearm it. Full magical suppression prevents sensing/operation while it lasts and does not queue missed triggers; duration continues. An acoustic silence can block only sound independently of a physically detected trigger. Resolve simultaneous eligible one-shot triggers in creation order then stable cast ID. Consume a triggered use before its payload, even when a later barrier, silence, immunity, cancellation or receiver state prevents useful output. A sound produced by another real spell can be a phonetic trigger if all actual constraints match, but each waiting trigger resolves at most once and cannot recreate itself or another charge. Source death/distance ends a nonconcentration effect only where the source/row says so; it does not refresh it. Suppression does not undo already completed mundane writing, moved matter, healing, damage or superficial preparation. An explicit persistent permanent spell remains a magical effect, not a manufactured item or free rechargeable device.

### LR-G13 — Copying, acceleration, delay and proxies

Cantrip Echo, Echo Cast, Quick Cast and Delay Spell are the only new wrapper identities authorized here. None may take itself or any of those four as payload, nor may an already echoed/released payload become a replay source. One payload may receive at most one of these temporal/replay modifications; they do not duplicate Ready or grant an additional Bonus Action, Reaction or full Action. Existing acquired class permissions remain their own exceptions, not automatically folded into this family. Select the actual original casting record, same ordinary source class and native legal access; only that spell's normal Action version with one finite resolution is eligible, excluding long casts, Reactions, Bonus Action spells and ongoing maintenance actions. Repeating a miss or resisted but fully resolved cast permits a fresh legal cast, not copying its damage result; a countered or aborted original is not a replay source. New targets/range/components and current costs/statistics must qualify at the new commitment. No active rider, successful roll, other caster's access, expended item charge or selected resource award is copied for free. Cantrip Echo's row defines one composite ordinary-cast transaction; Echo Cast/Quick Cast/Delay Spell instead have their separately paid arming transactions and later actual payload commitments. Record parent, wrapper, payload, cost, original owner and single-use consumption. No zero-base-cost replay recursively generates resource refunds or unearned casts. A legal fresh payload still resolves its own actual damage and expressly funded healing; this guard prevents copied old awards and recursive new-cast/refund grants, not the payload’s specified new outcome. Resonance Tether changes a legitimate delivery origin only, never the responsible caster or their RP/RC pool.

### LR-G14 — Reflection without a new cast

Spell Mirror and Soul Mirror intercept only the first actually resolving ordinary spell effect that has exactly their warded creature as its sole direct creature recipient, beneficial or hostile. Area, multitarget, self-only, anchor/object, summoning, channel/ongoing tick and already redirected effects are ineligible. NEW interception/return distinction: a Touch-delivered, healing or willing-recipient spell is eligible for interception when it satisfies the foregoing sole-recipient and scope requirements. The original caster's eligibility to receive the return, including actual consent and creature-type requirements, is tested only after that eligible ward has been spent. An ineligible original caster therefore causes a spent, unsuccessful return and leaves the original recipient, exactly as below. A legal Touch return uses this explicit magical return, not a new physical touch or Action. No rank cap is added; actual protective exceptions can still forbid redirection. After counterspell resolution but before that spell's attack/save/effect, spend the ward once and substitute the original actual caster as recipient only when it is presently a legal recipient under the payload's own type/other eligibility. The original caster remains owner and supplies DC/attack roll and costs; this creates no new casting, resources or second packet. An ineligible original caster causes the spent redirection to fail and the original spell continues against its original warded target, not automatic cancellation. An eligible redirected attack can miss normally; spend is not refunded. The original targeting distance has already been established; the explicit return uses that same causal cast, with return line blocked by an actual barrier defeating the return path, not arbitrary remote range extension. A blocked return likewise leaves the original target. A causal root can be redirected at most once by these two spells; another mirror on the new target neither redirects it again nor consumes its ward. Duplicate wards on the original target arbitrate earliest creation then stable ID; only the selected ward spends. These are NEW finite interpretations of “first/single-target spell,” not permissions for Wizard/Oracle or other reflected-damage features to use this protocol.

### LR-G15 — Necrotic damage, healing, corpses and restrictions

Actual damage and healing follow completed Core and feat procedures. No ordinary necrotic spell gains Bleeding, disease, Oracle Decay, a curse, corpse fuel, a soul resource or a healing prohibition by name/tag. A drain's specific permitted funding is stated in its row; an unfunded heal/RP transfer cannot be manufactured from overkill, a reflected packet, an already paid award, target transfer, current permission overriding no-resource origin, or a temporary body. Existing Lifedraw, Vampiric Touch, Lifeleech, Necrotic Affinity, Soul Cage feat and class drains keep their own nonidentical source predicates; the new drain restrictions are not retrospective amendments to them. Actual independently eligible acquired feats can respond once under their completed source rules, never a second time to the resulting heal/resource transfer. A corpse interface uses actual dead identity and retained memory; it does not establish an afterlife, soul custody, truth beyond memory or current thoughts. Preserve actual death/removal clocks, condition and no-resource restrictions through stabilization, question, animation and return. A later paid question cannot retrieve information the old remains never retained; fragments are one donor history, not separate minds.

### LR-G16 — Real matter, preparations and economy

A spell moving or reshaping real matter retains the actual predecessor quantity, material, contents, owner, damage and consumed/harvest history. Count retained mass and disposed matter once. It does not change source tier, named ingredient eligibility, equipment grade, formula knowledge, rarity, assigned value, an old recovery claim or an apparatus's paid service/charges. Cosmetic temperature/color/shape does not sterilize food, undo spoilage, neutralize a poison or supply a professional crafting result. Explicit elementary structures allowed below still need real support and dimensions; no unknown material or hidden load-bearing property is authenticated by the spell. A genuine subsequently usable mundane item can enter only its actual existing rules and finite market; no automatic sale price, buyer, XP or crafting-output multiplication. Fulfilled 687/433 recipe and NWX/LA procedures remain controlling.

### LR-G17 — Concentration and early termination

Where a row says concentration, it occupies the one actual concentration slot, ends under the completed damage-save/incapacitation rules and can be ended voluntarily under that rule; no new per-round maintenance RP is imposed. A source explicitly printing concentration keeps it even when an independent rider ends sooner. NEW voluntary early termination for a nonconcentration instance authored below requires the original caster's Action and actual perception of its anchor/recipient within that spell's original range; no remote recall or refund. Instant completed consequences cannot be voluntarily reversed. Named permanent Until Dispelled effects have no general voluntary end from this default; use their own source or actual dispel. Invalid physical continued operation removes only what can no longer function; full destruction of a required anchor ends its anchored instance. No repaired anchor is a new instance.

### LR-G18 — Aliases and unassigned variants

Uncontrollable Laughter uses the matching historical Tasha's Hideous Laughter Core metadata only through the explicit IL-18 binding; Resonance Tether analogously uses Mana Tether through UN-13. Preserve both historical names, exact effects and alias records without adding separate ordinary identities. Prestidigitation's two actual school modes are independently specified in IL-06 and selected by the cast's actual access row. Phantasmal Force rank 3, Stone Shape rank 4 and Mislead rank 5 remain preserved unassigned alternative records: no current classAccess row acquires those higher-rank modes, no chooser can buy them by paying more RP, and no new question or ordinary promotion is invented for them. The assigned lower-rank modes receive complete directions. Echo Cast's Core “base RP” is the chosen operational reading of its catalog's broader shorthand. Soul Tap's catalog HP AND Resonance selects the joint operation explicitly. Unravel Spell's “Tier 4” means the generating ordinary spell's rank≤4, not material/equipment/creature tier. All differences stay beside unchanged competing source fields.

### LR-G19 — Audience and comparison records

All 137 selected ordinary identities and 141 original book bindings remain preserved internally; public teaching uses only actual public fields and the resulting operational mode when later exactly reviewed. A source mention does not publish a private dossier, class membership, material source or hidden research metadata. The 54 full selected class comparisons and 114 distinct spell counterparts retain their actual IDs, resource engines, windows and statuses; matching names/words do not merge any of them. Pending feats and spell comparisons stay pending. The eight submitted proposals are separately disposed below as source directions, not adopted by their mere inclusion. No change to the fulfilled language, teaching or recipe authority is required by these ordinary directions.

### LR-G20 — Delivery and deferred receipts

R3-04 Blink is receipt-only: retain its full original and question, current pending operational state and literal setting references; author no plane itinerary, probability, reappearance/body procedure or promotion. Other incidental literal names, including Silent Ones, Heartcell, Matrix, Voice and deep-language terms when present, remain source receipts without interpretation. Local portals/teleports below are explicitly local procedures, not a decision about cosmology. Source authority here does not certify gameplay runtime, human teaching/balance, bestiary/acquisition availability, DD01 or final systemic consistency. Owner's final whole-game flow precedes an authorized GitHub push; verified remote commit precedes careful cosmology review. No deletion/push is authorized.

### IL-01 — Flickerstep

Identity: `flickerstep`. Source decision: `LR-IL-01`. Status: **reviewed-authored-operational-playtest**.

| Actual class access | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Bard | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Crimson Agent | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Monk | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Mirrorsage | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Thief | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Wizard | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 30 feet

**Components.** V, S

**Targets and mode.** One willing creature within 30 ft, including self.

**Duration.** Until the caster’s next regular-turn start, or the next legal attack committed against the recipient, whichever occurs first.

**Resolution.** Automatic willing-target benefit; no delivery attack/save.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** Give +1 AC against that one committed attack, selected automatically by chronology rather than after seeing its roll. Spend at legal attack commitment before the attack roll, including when it is subsequently canceled or misses. It supplies neither movement nor a displaced actual body.

**Limits and ending.** Compare +1 with other applicable flat AC bonuses under LR-G05; retain real cover and independent applicable effects in their own families. A canceled uncommitted declaration spends nothing. Current suppression prevents the bonus and consumption, not expiry.

**Numerical parameters.**

```json
  "acBonus": 1,
  "targetCount": 1,

  "uses": 1
}
```

**Use-time prerequisite.** Actual legal access/repertoire, current source and target state, components, range/line, time and resources under the stated finite procedure; these are use-time facts, not missing source-conference questions.

**Authored example (Authored expectation, not executed runtime or campaign authentication).** AC 13 with this +1 and an applicable stronger +5 flat ward uses the permitted stronger ward, not +6. The first committed attack still spends Flickerstep; a later miss or cancellation does not restore it.

**Source-authoring notice.** Every concrete target bound, resistance, unsupported metadata replacement, mode, action/clock/lifecycle detail and numerical default not printed in the preserved original is NEW source-authoring direction, never a claim that it was printed. ### IL-02 — Silent Echo

Identity: `silent-echo`. Source decision: `LR-IL-02`. Status: **reviewed-authored-operational-playtest**.

| Actual class access | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Bard | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Crimson Agent | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Monk | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Mirrorsage | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Thief | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Wizard | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 10 feet

**Components.** V, S

**Targets and mode.** One actual recent heard phrase; one perceived sound origin within 10 ft.

**Duration.** One playback lasting no more than 6 seconds; no stored effect.

**Resolution.** Automatic sound reproduction; observers retain actual hearing/knowledge and LR-G07 examination.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** At declaration select the most recent complete phrase heard by the caster from an origin within 10 ft in the previous minute, before this cast’s own V. Maximum playback length is six seconds. For a longer actual utterance, select its last complete word or contiguous bounded phrase segment that was genuinely heard and fits six seconds; do not reconstruct an unheard ending. If two phrases truly finish simultaneously, choose one actual qualifying phrase. Replay its heard phonemes, cadence and vocal quality once at ordinary conversational loudness from the selected legal point. Incomplete/inaudible parts are not reconstructed.

**Limits and ending.** No language knowledge is required to reproduce the sounds and none is gained. Another playback can be the actual heard sample, never authentication or a fidelity upgrade. Acoustics, silence, walls and perception remain real. Recasting needs a currently qualifying sample and a new paid cast/cadence event. This is not the speaker, an interpreter, V, a credential or a documented control channel.

**Numerical parameters.**

```json
{
  "rangeFeet": 10,
  "sampleLookbackSeconds": 60,
  "maximumPlaybackSeconds": 6
}
```

**Shared rules.** LR-G01, LR-G02, LR-G03, LR-G04, LR-G05, LR-G06, LR-G07, LR-G08, LR-G09, LR-G10, LR-G11, LR-G12, LR-G13, LR-G14, LR-G15, LR-G16, LR-G17, LR-G18, LR-G19, LR-G20.

**Source-mode bindings.**

```json

    "sourceSchool": "Illusion",
    "sourceRank": 0,
    "sourceName": "Silent Echo",
    "sourceCastingTime": "1 action",
    "sourceRange": "10 feet",
    "sourceDuration": "Instantaneous",
    "sourceComponents": "V, S",
    "sourceEffect": "Mimics last word or phrase said within 10 ft.",
    "binding": "ASSIGNED_MODE_WITH_EXPLICIT_AUTHORING_COMPLETION"
  }
]
```

**Use-time prerequisite.** Actual legal access/repertoire, current source and target state, components, range/line, time and resources under the stated finite procedure; these are use-time facts, not missing source-conference questions.

**Authored example (Authored expectation, not executed runtime or campaign authentication).** An Aquan phrase heard four seconds ago can be repeated without understanding it. If silence prevented hearing the final word, the spell cannot recover that word from an asserted transcript.

**Source-authoring notice.** Every concrete target bound, resistance, unsupported metadata replacement, mode, action/clock/lifecycle detail and numerical default not printed in the preserved original is NEW source-authoring direction, never a claim that it was printed. Explicit retained clauses and LR-G KEEP rules are not rewritten.

### IL-03 — Illusive Flash

Identity: `illusive-flash`. Source decision: `LR-IL-03`. Status: **reviewed-authored-operational-playtest**.

| Actual class access | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Bard | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Crimson Agent | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Monk | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Mirrorsage | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Thief | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |
| Wizard | 0 | 1 | Illusion | 0 |

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 30 feet

**Components.** V, S

**Targets and mode.** One perceived enemy creature within 30 ft that can receive the visual flash.

**Duration.** Hit mark lasts until the end of that creature’s next regular turn, or its next qualifying attack roll.

**Resolution.** Ranged spell attack versus AC; miss applies no mark and deals no damage.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** On hit, impose disadvantage on the target’s next attack roll relying on ordinary visual perception before expiry. The delivery roll is the caster’s separate ranged spell attack, not the beneficiary’s marked attack. No damage or Blinded condition is created.

Universal access selects one instantaneous cleaning/soiling of loose harmless surface dust on 1 square foot using only actual present dust, a harmless momentary scent/spark display in a 6-inch cube, or a nonverbal sound of at most one second. ### IL-15 — False Footsteps

Identity: `false-footsteps`. Source decision: `LR-IL-15`. Status: **reviewed-authored-operational-playtest**.

| Actual class access | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| Crimson Agent | 1 | 2 | Illusion | 2 |
| Monk | 1 | 3 | Illusion | 2 |
| Mirrorsage | 1 | 1 | Illusion | 2 |
| Thief | 1 | 3 | Illusion | 2 |
| Wizard | 1 | 1 | Illusion | 2 |

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 30 feet

**Components.** V, S, F

**Targets and mode.** One fixed predeclared sound path wholly within 30 ft of casting origin.

**Duration.** 3 rounds, ending at the caster’s third future regular-turn start; no concentration.

**Resolution.** Automatic sound, no save/compelled pursuit.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** NEW path consists of up to three straight linked segments totaling at most 30 ft over actual known walking surfaces. Select direction and familiar cadence when casting. Resolve exactly three scheduled passes, each up to six seconds: the first when the effect manifests, then one at each of the caster’s next two future regular-turn starts. Its existing expiry is the third future regular-turn start measured from manifestation. These are three instance-specific checkpoints, not a separate once-per-numbered-round allowance; two checkpoints can fall in one numbered round when manifestation occurred before the caster’s regular turn. An extra turn creates no pass or expiry checkpoint. For a normally cast spell, manifestation is its resolved effect; for a legal generic Ready hold, manifestation is release, not paid preparation. No sound is emitted merely by preparing the hold. Actual acoustic obstruction and suppression still apply at each checkpoint; a suppressed scheduled pass is spent, not queued. The path cannot be remotely redirected.

**Limits and ending.** No tracks, pressure, combatant or automatic stealth result. This is the ranked three-round fixed pattern, not Phantom Footsteps’ one pass or Ghost Sound’s separate permission. Actual hearing and LR-G07 examination apply; suppression spends scheduled passes without queued repeats.

**Numerical parameters.**

```json
{
  "rangeFeet": 30,
  "pathFeet": 30,
  "segments": 3,
  "passes": 3
}
```

**Shared rules.** LR-G01, LR-G02, LR-G03, LR-G04, LR-G05, LR-G06, LR-G07, LR-G08, LR-G09, LR-G10, LR-G11, LR-G12, LR-G13, LR-G14, LR-G15, LR-G16, LR-G17, LR-G18, LR-G19, LR-G20.

**Authored example (Authored expectation, not executed runtime or campaign authentication).** The first pass occurs at effect manifestation; the next two occur at the next two future caster regular-turn starts, and the instance expires at the third. A legal readied release before that caster’s regular turn can place the first and second passes in the same numbered round. Extra turns add no checkpoint or fourth pass; suppression spends the scheduled pass without a catch-up.

The actual holder needs simple-melee proficiency to add their PB; another proficiency/caster ability is not granted. Criticals double its two weapon dice normally. Attack ownership is the actual wielder, not a second caster spell-hit claim.

**Limits and ending.** The blade occupies a real hand/carry slot and can be handed over or dropped through ordinary handling; it does not return automatically. Suppression makes the blade unusable, retaining the anchored instance/location and continuing deadline; it resumes only at its actual position if the remaining effect is lawful. Meld makes it inaccessible. At HP 0, expiry or concentration end it disappears with no recoverable matter. No repair, value, ammo, body/stat grant, extra attack or added cantrip scaling.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. ### UN-14 — Delay Spell

| Wizard | 1 | 1 | Universal | 2 |
| Paladin | 1 | 2 | Universal | 2 |

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** Self arm; preserved 30-ft metadata does not capture another caster’s effect or replace the payload’s native range.

**Components.** V, S, F

**Targets and mode.** The caster’s next eligible own native Action spell, not another temporal/replay wrapper.

**Duration.** Waiting arm until the end of the caster’s next regular turn; one held payload for no more than 6 elapsed seconds.

**Resolution.** Arming automatic; payload resolves its actual native roll/effect at the fixed release checkpoint.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** NEW arming costs the printed Action and ordinary price; no free same-turn extra Action. Before the later payload commits, declare one future release checkpoint no later than the caster’s next regular start/6-second equivalent. Pay and counter the payload once at its actual casting commitment, lock the original recipients/point and selections, and hold its effect under the caster’s concentration. Holding consumes the waiting arm. Release is automatic at the declared checkpoint, not a new Action, Reaction or casting; no optional post-roll retiming. The caster must retain the holding concentration and necessary current ability to release.

**Limits and ending.** Revalidate the locked targets, native range/line from the caster’s actual current position, physical geometry and mandatory eligibility at release; invalid parts fail, no replacement target is selected. The held payload cannot outlive six seconds. If payload itself requires concentration, the same single slot continues into it; its actual effect-duration clock starts on release. Another concentration or suppression preventing release loses the payload, not its costs. It does not capture an enemy spell, duplicate Ready, permit a second Counterspell casting window or pause the world.

**Range.** Touch

**Components.** V, S, F

**Targets and mode.** One actual touched noncreature anchor; willing holder if attended.

Choose first subsequent creature touch or one specified audible phonetic word spoken within 10 ft and reaching the anchor. The creating contact/V cannot trigger the new thread. Record the trigger, output and actual source; spend before output.

**Limits and ending.** Maximum three waiting threads per caster, one on an exact anchor; a fourth successful creation ends the oldest waiting thread. No stored parent spell, damage, active listener intelligence, language test, identity/moral detector, reset, reusable charge or free component. Actual sound/physical contact and LR-G12 ordering/suppression apply. Full suppression misses events without queue; acoustic silence alone can prevent the word/chime while a physical touch can still spend the use. Transfer/death/distance do not refresh waiting state.

**Range.** Self preparation; preserve original Core 30 ft as historical metadata, not ally acceleration.

**Components.** V, S, F

**Targets and mode.** The caster’s next eligible native Action ordinary spell on a later regular turn.

**Duration.** Until the end of the caster’s next regular turn; one use, no concentration.

**Resolution.** Automatic preparation and later one-action-type conversion.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. On the caster’s next regular turn, one eligible own native Action spell may instead be committed with that turn’s actual Bonus Action, paying all its actual costs/components and recording a separate ordinary cast. Consume Quick Cast at that payload commitment even if countered/missed. No rank cap is invented beyond actual access/repertoire.

**Limits and ending.** No benefit on an extra turn, Reaction, ritual, long cast, maintenance or another wrapper. It grants neither a second Bonus Action nor an extra full Action allowance. If the next regular turn’s Bonus Action is spent on a companion command, there is no free accelerated spell; the arm expires at that turn’s end.

| Actual class access | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Bard | 2 | 4 | Universal | 3 |
| Cleric | 2 | 3 | Universal | 3 |
| Mage | 2 | 3 | Universal | 3 |
| Mirrorsage | 2 | 4 | Universal | 3 |
| Tinker | 2 | 6 | Universal | 3 |
| Wizard | 2 | 3 | Universal | 3 |
| Paladin | 2 | 5 | Universal | 3 |

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 30 feet

**Components.** V, S, F

**Targets and mode.** The caster’s most recent fully resolved eligible native Action ordinary spell of actual rank 0–2, cast since the beginning of the previous regular turn.

**Duration.** NEW exact next-round window: record the numbered round in which the Echo Cast arming spell is committed, including paid generic Ready preparation. The replay is available only during the caster’s regular turn in the immediately following numbered round and expires at that turn’s end. A still-upcoming regular turn in the commitment round and every extra turn are ineligible. Outside initiative, or through a transition, retain that scheduled next-round regular-turn window under the existing virtual-clock mapping; do not start a fresh window at release or on entering initiative. No concentration is required for a manifested arm; an actual generic Ready hold retains its own concentration and earlier expiry.

**Resolution.** Arming automatic; replay uses the payload’s fresh native resolution.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** NEW Echo Cast is its own paid Action casting. Select the actual original source record at that commitment. During the one regular-turn replay window specified in Duration, after the arming effect has actually manifested, the caster may use their actual Action to repeat that same ordinary spell through the same class access, with fresh legal targets/selections, actual components and current statistics. An unmanifested, expired or lost arm grants no replay, and losing the scheduled opportunity never rolls it forward. Waive only the payload’s base RP, choosing Core’s precise “base” wording over the catalog shorthand; retain real additional/strain/material costs and ordinary cadence/concentration. The payload’s native range remains, with a non-Self selected endpoint capped at 30 ft for this wrapper.

**Limits and ending.** The arm grants no extra Action/Bonus Action. It is consumed at replay commitment, not on a successful outcome. A failed original attack/save can be replayed; a countered/aborted original cannot. Exclude LR-G13 wrappers/replays and copied resource benefits. Starting another concentration at replay obeys the ordinary one-slot rule; nothing is carried from the original paid effect except the spell identity authorization.

**Numerical parameters.**

```json
{
  "maximumPayloadRank": 2,
  "replays": 1,
  "baseRpWaived": true,
  "additionalRpWaived": false,
  "endpointCapFeet": 30
}
```

**Shared rules.** LR-G01, LR-G02, LR-G03, LR-G04, LR-G05, LR-G06, LR-G07, LR-G08, LR-G09, LR-G10, LR-G11, LR-G12, LR-G13, LR-G14, LR-G15, LR-G16, LR-G17, LR-G18, LR-G19, LR-G20.

```json
    "sourceRank": 2,
    "sourceName": "Echo Cast",
    "sourceCastingTime": "1 action",
    "sourceRange": "30 feet",
    "sourceDuration": "Instantaneous",

  }
```

**Source conflict disposition.** Core base-RP waiver is controlling operational choice; preserve catalog broader RP wording.

**Source conflict disposition.** Tier 2 binds actual ordinary rank≤2, not equipment or material tier.

**Use-time prerequisite.** Actual legal access/repertoire, current source and target state, components, range/line, time and resources under the stated finite procedure; these are use-time facts, not missing source-conference questions.

**Authored example (Authored expectation, not executed runtime or campaign authentication).** A rank-2 payload with base 3 RP and a lawful +1 strain surcharge replays for 1 RP, not zero. The separate Echo Cast arming price remains spent and cannot itself be the copied payload.

### UN-23 — Magic Mouth

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 30 feet

**Components.** V, S, F

**Targets and mode.** One actual object within 30 ft; willing holder if attended.

**Duration.** Waiting up to 24 hours; one playback lasting no more than 6 seconds. No concentration.

**Resolution.** Automatic one-use sound on one predeclared actual trigger.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** Adopt the supplied proposal: during casting the caster records their own actually spoken message of at most six seconds, obeying the aggregate speech budget; no imaginary extra recording time. Choose first subsequent creature touch, one phonetic keyword heard at the object from within 10 ft, or the first actual visible creature crossing from outside into 5 ft of it. The visual mode requires ordinary light/line of sight and grants no special detection of Invisible creatures. The creating contact/recording/V cannot trigger the new mouth. Spend the use before replaying at ordinary conversation volume.

**Limits and ending.** Maximum three waiting mouths per caster and one on the same exact anchor; a fourth successful creation ends the oldest waiting instance. It interprets no meaning, morality, password ownership, identity or secret thought. Acoustic silence blocks hearing/output as applicable but a real touch can still consume the use silently; full suppression misses triggers without queue. Replays are not the caster’s later V, a device controller or guaranteed authentication. Transfer/repair/caster death grants no new use.

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** Touch

**Components.** V, S

**Targets and mode.** One touched mundane noncreature object ≤10 lb fitting a 1-ft cube, or one ≤1-square-foot exposed patch of a larger mundane object/armor; willing holder/wearer if attended.

**Duration.** 1 round, ending at the caster’s next regular start; no concentration.

**Resolution.** Automatic temporary hardening.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. **Effect.** NEW for an ordinary nonarmor object, raise that selected object/patch’s structural AC by 1 against direct attacks on it. For actually worn manufactured armor, instead grant its wearer the printed +1 AC while that treated armor remains worn; do not also raise the whole armor’s structural AC by this cast. Natural creature armor is not an object. The larger armor need not fit a one-foot cube, but the touched treatment patch must fit the square-foot limit.

**Limits and ending.** No HP healing, durability maximum, resistance, stone material, new grade, weapon damage or magical-host reconstruction. Flat wearer AC uses the existing strongest-bonus family, not +1 per armor patch. Unworn armor provides no wearer benefit and changing wearer does not move the cast’s original recipient benefit.

Identity: `absorb-elements`. Source decision: `LR-TR-17`. Status: **reviewed-authored-operational-playtest**.

| Thief | 1 | 3 | Transmutation | 2 |
| Tinker | 1 | 2 | Transmutation | 2 |
| Wizard | 1 | 1 | Transmutation | 2 |

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** Self preparation; original Core 30 ft retained without a remote ward grant.

**Components.** V, S, F

**Targets and mode.** Self only; prepare before the incoming damage event.

**Duration.** Prepared ward until the caster’s next regular start; resistance ends then; stored attack rider until the end of the caster’s next regular turn. No concentration.

**Resolution.** Automatic first eligible incoming-damage interception after a legal Action cast.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** NEW keep Core one-Action casting rather than importing a Reaction. At casting fix an ordered preference among acid, cold, fire, lightning, poison and thunder. The first subsequent incoming event with positive pre-defense damage of one listed type triggers the ward; for a mixed event select its earliest type in that fixed order. Give resistance to that type’s triggering component and further damage of that type until the next caster regular start. Consume the ward’s triggering capacity once. Prepare +1d6 of that selected type for the caster’s next actual attack roll before the rider deadline; on a hit add it once, on a miss lose it.

**Limits and ending.** No retrospective reduction of damage already resolved, no immediate extra attack, new Bonus Action/Reaction, element reservoir or resource refund. Zero actual damage after immunity still consumes a triggering event selected from positive pre-defense damage; the bounded rider is not HP/RP funding. Only the selected type changes; physical or other typed damage is untouched. An attack’s critical doubles the rider die normally. The existing one-bonus-die-spell limit applies.

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 30 feet

**Components.** V, S, F

**Targets and mode.** One perceived Small/Medium/Large creature or nonunique object within 30 ft with actual compatible surrounding space; willing or CON save negates.

**Duration.** Concentration, up to 1 minute.

**Resolution.** CON negates unwilling transformation; automatic for willing eligible target.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** NEW interpret50% as linear scaling: grow to1.5× original linear dimensions or shrink to0.5×. Temporary body/compatible carried-gear volume and weight factors are27/8 or1/8 respectively, tracked as a reversible magical manifestation over the same original matter, not new harvestable mass. NEW the target has at most one Enlarge/Reduce size effect across casters: a newly resolved application replaces the prior size record and calculates from the current form’s non-Enlarge/Reduce baseline, never an already scaled snapshot. A genuine change of underlying form ends this size effect before admitting the new form. No momentary replacement reversion may crush or displace another body. Movement Speed, HP, abilities, weapon dice and proficiency do not change by inference. Use one adjacent size category at most where actual geometry requires it; size-category labels never override measured fit.

**Limits and ending.** Before commitment verify the enlarged body/gear can occupy its full shape without crushing/displacement; hidden impossibility causes failed effect after payment, not knowledge. Equipment that cannot legally follow the body uses the completed meld/placement/host rules rather than being duplicated or forced into an incompatible form. On ending restore original dimensions/mass with current actual damage/resources, using ordinary pending-placement rules if the location no longer fits. Cutting off, consuming or trading enlarged matter cannot keep the magical surplus or reset parent history.

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 30 feet

**Components.** V, S, M/F

**Targets and mode.** One actual substantial corpse/remains anchor within 30 ft, with a recorded death within the previous24elapsed hours.

**Duration.** One question issued during casting and one response begun immediately afterward, lasting at most 6 seconds. No concentration.

**Resolution.** Automatic retained-memory interface after actual eligibility; no save, deception or compulsion of a living mind.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** NEW one Death Whisper interview per actual death event across all casters, consumed at legal interview commitment even if the question yields unknown or its answer is unheard. The caster asks one actually spoken question fitting their available speech time. The remains provide one truthful subjective-memory answer of at most 20words and at most 6 seconds, in an actual language the deceased knew; no translation is supplied. No intact mouth is required if substantial actual retained remains/memory qualify, but ashes/no recorded memory do not invent an answer.

**Limits and ending.** No new memories from a later resurrected life appear in old fragments. Active undead, currently living identities and disappeared/no-resource bodies are ineligible. Protected/unknown information yields unknown/unavailable, not lore fabrication. Paid Lesser/regular Speak with Dead retain their own independent question allowances; this cantrip’s once-per-death limit is not a retroactive cap on them. This is a memory-interface choice, not soul contact or custody.

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 60 feet

**Components.** V, S, F

**Targets and mode.** One actually willing creature within 60 ft, informed of the ending Stun before accepting.

**Duration.** Concentration, up to 1 minute; ending backlash one hostile round.

**Resolution.** Automatic benefits; no save against the voluntarily accepted source cost, subject to actual Stunned immunity.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. **Effect.** KEEP+2 AC, doubled existing locomotion Speed and one extra FULL Action opportunity, not an imported narrow action menu. NEW grant/use that extra Action only on the recipient’s regular turn, within the existing one-extra-full-Action slot per creature/numbered round. Mid-turn application can make the slot available only if it is genuinely unspent. A cast originating from an extra full Action cannot create a new extra full Action that round. No added Bonus Action/Reaction, spell knowledge or resource refill; ordinary costs/components still apply to an Action spell chosen for that slot.

**Limits and ending.** NEW ending-cost exception: actual termination by expiry, lost concentration, dispel, replacement or dismissal inflicts Stunned until the end of the recipient’s next future regular turn, even if this caster’s hostile hard-control allowance was already spent. This is an explicitly accepted voluntary buff cost, not a hostile hard-control claim/refund; actual Stunned immunity still prevents it. Temporary suppression removes benefits without triggering the cost until actual termination; it does not refresh spent action/movement. Replacing an old Haste triggers its ending cost once, not evasion; if that incapacitates the source caster, ordinary concentration consequences can abort the new cast. Declining the ending risk makes the willing-only target ineligible.

### R3-21 — Glyph of Warding

Identity: `glyph-of-warding`. Source decision: `LR-R3-21`. Status: **reviewed-authored-operational-playtest**.

| Actual class access | Rank | Minimum class level | School | Base RP |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

| Cleric | 3 | 5 | Universal | 5 |
| Mage | 3 | 5 | Universal | 5 |
| Mirrorsage | 3 | 7 | Universal | 5 |
| Tinker | 3 | 10 | Universal | 5 |
| Wizard | 3 | 5 | Universal | 5 |
| Paladin | 3 | 9 | Universal | 5 |

Additional RP cost: 0. Acquisition and the actual class engine still apply.

**Casting time.** 1 action

**Range.** 60 feet

**Components.** V, S, M/F

**Targets and mode.** One actual writable noncreature surface≤1square foot within 60 ft; willing holder if attended.

**Duration.** Printed concentration up to 1 minute, one trigger.

**Resolution.** Automatic inscription; one explicitly selected finite payload at its trigger.

**Cost and access.** Use the actual acquired class grant and its preserved classAccess row for rank, minimum class level, school and base RP. Apply that class’s completed casting engine, current repeat history, discounts, Dissonance surcharge and this identity’s additionalRpCost; use LR-G01, LR-G02 and the explicit payload/wrapper exceptions in this card. Oracle RC is separate. Sufficient resources do not grant acquisition. Pay committed costs even on a miss, successful resistance or counter. There is no generic upcast or level scaling.

**Effect.** NEW choose one payload: alarm—ordinary audible chime≤6 seconds; repulsion—the triggering creature makes STR save or is pushed5 ft directly away from the glyph along a legal supported path, no damage; ward—the triggering willing creature receives+2 AC against its next committed attack before the caster’s next regular start. Choose one trigger: first subsequent direct bodily creature touch of the marked surface, or first actually visible creature entry from outside into5 ft of it. Initial inscription/contact never triggers it. The glyph has ordinary visual perception only in the entry mode; no hidden identity/moral filter. Consume before the one payload, retain original caster/DC/ownership, and release the glyph concentration after that payload resolves.

**Limits and ending.** This is not stored spell access, a separate free casting, damage by name, permanent trap or Rune Carving item. No new payloadRP cost is added beyond its actual source casting because this finite minor payload is the spell’s chosen effect; no recharge/retrigger. The same actual anchor can move only with its real custody/geometry, with trigger history retained; full suppression misses events without queue. Alarm needs actual acoustics; repulsion stops before occupied/unsafe/blocked space without crushing. The ward uses normal flatAC/one-use arbitration and grants no different card’s ability.
